COST OF DELAY (assumptions: video held L s before the first synced frame; buffer = 720p video ~2.0 Mbps (0.25 MB/s) + dub audio ~32 kbps + source-audio buffer ~0.01 MB/s, total ~0.26 MB/s; 1080p 5 Mbps would be ~2.5x the video part):
  L=15 s: startup wait 15 s; buffer ~3.9 MB (720p) / ~9.5 MB (1080p)
  L=20 s: startup wait 20 s; buffer ~5.2 MB (720p) / ~12.7 MB (1080p)
  L=25 s: startup wait 25 s; buffer ~6.5 MB (720p) / ~15.9 MB (1080p)
  L=30 s: startup wait 30 s; buffer ~7.8 MB (720p) / ~19.1 MB (1080p)
  L=40 s: startup wait 40 s; buffer ~10.4 MB (720p) / ~25.4 MB (1080p)
  L=60 s: startup wait 60 s; buffer ~15.6 MB (720p) / ~38.1 MB (1080p)
  NOTE: in-sim, extra L beyond readiness buys no sync (windows are L-independent); its only cost is the wait + buffer above, its only benefit is readiness slack for unusually long/late bursts.
